# BIBLIA DEL MUNDO — TOY STORY
## Documento de referencia para *El Comisario de Blackwater*
### Compilado por Sofía Medina · 23 de septiembre de 2026

> **Cómo leer este documento.** La Parte I es análisis y canon verificado,
> escrito a mano. Los Apéndices contienen el guion de *Toy Story* (1995) en
> su versión limpia, los subtítulos de las tres primeras películas y el
> artículo enciclopédico de referencia: el oído literal de la franquicia.
> Regla de oro: la Parte I decide *qué* significa el mundo; los Apéndices
> muestran *cómo habla*.

---

## §1. LAS REGLAS DEL MUNDO DE LOS JUGUETES

El universo Toy Story no es magia declarada: es un sistema con leyes
internas tan estrictas como las de un western. Escribirlas bien es lo que
separa un crossover serio de una parodia.

**R1. La vida secreta.** Los juguetes están vivos cuando ningún humano los
observa. No es una elección: es el estado natural. La "inmovilidad" es la
excepción, y es obligatoria.

**R2. El congelamiento.** Ante la presencia humana, todo juguete cae en
inmovilidad absoluta. La evidencia de la franquicia (Buzz al llegar, que cree
ser un ranger real y aun así se congela al entrar Andy; Forky en TS4; los
Buzz defectuosos de fábrica en TS5, que se congelan sin comprender por qué)
demuestra que no es una regla social sino una *programación corporal
involuntaria*: el cuerpo obedece aunque la mente no lo entienda.

**R3. La regla se puede romper (y cuesta).** En el clímax de TS1, Woody y los
juguetes mutantes de Sid se mueven frente a un humano para salvar a Buzz.
Woody lo dice explícito: "vamos a tener que romper las reglas". Interpretación
canónica coherente: el congelamiento es un bloqueo mental inconsciente; un
juguete que *acepta plenamente lo que es* puede, por pura fuerza de voluntad,
apagar el interruptor. Por eso Buzz (confundido sobre su identidad) no podía,
y Woody (seguro de sí) sí. Romper la regla es un acto de identidad y de
sacrificio: después de hacerlo, nada vuelve a ser igual.

**R4. La identidad es el motor.** Buzz descubre que es un juguete viendo un
comercial de televisión: la escena es, en palabras de la crítica (Nicholas
Barber, BBC), literatura de Philip K. Dick: "atrapado en un ambiente hostil,
un hombre descubre que todo lo que sabe es mentira; no es el héroe que asumió
que era". Todo personaje de la franquicia enfrenta alguna versión de esa
crisis: Buzz (¿ranger o juguete?), Woody (¿favorito o reemplazo?), Jessie
(¿amada o abandonada?), Stinky Pete (¿pieza de museo o juguete?), Forky
(¿tenedor o juguete?), Gabby Gabby (¿defectuosa o digna de amor?).

**R5. El propósito: el niño.** Un juguete existe *para* un niño. La lealtad
al dueño no es servidumbre: es teología. Woody la predica ("Andy nos
necesita"), la sufre (celos de Buzz) y la trasciende (TS3–TS4: elegir a
Bonnie, y luego elegir la vida "perdida" con Bo). Un juguete sin niño es un
fantasma con costuras.

**R6. El reemplazo es la muerte social.** Cumpleaños, mudanzas, cajas de
donación, vitrinas de museo, guarderías: toda la franquicia es una
meditación sobre ser sustituido. Los juguetes viejos frente a los nuevos es
el espejo exacto del Oeste viejo frente al nuevo en RDR2. Este es el puente
temático de *El Comisario de Blackwater*.

**R7. La comunidad.** Los juguetes funcionan como pueblo: asambleas (la
reunión con el baby-monitor en TS1), códigos, juicios (Woody acusado de
empujar a Buzz), exilios y regresos. Woody es su sheriff natural: no por
fuerza, por costumbre de cuidar.

**R8. El daño es real.** Un juguete puede romperse, perder piezas, ser
reparado (con cicatrices de pegamento), mutilado (Sid), destruido (la
incineradora de TS3). El dolor no se muestra como agonía humana sino como
pánico funcional — y aun así conmueve más.

**R9. Los juguetes no envejecen; los niños sí.** La tragedia de fondo: Andy
crece. El tiempo del juguete es circular (esperar, jugar, esperar); el del
niño, lineal. El amor perfecto del juguete es, por diseño, un amor que será
dejado atrás. TS3 lo convierte en liturgia: "gracias, chicos".

**R10. Lo que un juguete nunca hace.** Mentir sobre su naturaleza frente a
humanos (salvo el sacrificio de R3), abandonar a otro juguete en peligro
("jamás te va a abandonar" es la definición de Woody que da Andy), y —en el
código de Woody— dejar que un niño llore por un juguete que sí podía haber
salvado.

## §2. EL REPARTO — DOSSIERS

### WOODY (Sheriff Woody Pride)
- **Qué es:** muñeco de vaquero de trapo y pintura, con cuerda trasera que
  activa frases del viejo programa *Woody's Roundup*. Sin mecanismo caro, sin
  láser, sin botón: el juguete más "pobre" del cuarto y su líder natural.
- **Personalidad:** lealtad feroz, liderazgo por cuidado, terquedad que roza
  la soberbia, celos que lo avergüenzan, humor seco, habla solo cuando
  piensa, ingenio de superviviente (usa lo que tenga a mano: cuerdas, cajas,
  cohetes). Cuando se enoja de verdad, baja la voz.
- **Arco franquicia:** de sheriff del cuarto a hombre libre: aprende que el
  amor no es posesión del puesto; que ser querido no es ser único; que al
  final el propósito no lo da el dueño sino la elección.
- **Frases ancla:** "¡Hay una serpiente en mi bota!"; "You've got a friend
  in me" (como credo, no como canción); "Andy nos necesita"; "vamos a tener
  que romper las reglas"; el discurso final de Andy sobre él: "valiente como
  debe ser un vaquero, listo y gentil... jamás te va a abandonar".
- **Eco en el AU:** James "Woody" Pride calca el esqueleto: funda vacía
  (penitencia), lazo (la cuerda), lealtad a Arthur (el niño que no es niño),
  hablar solo, "una serpiente en mi bota" = Micah, liderazgo por cuidado,
  celos que lo hacen errar (cap. 7), voz que baja cuando se enoja.

### BUZZ LIGHTYEAR
- **Qué es:** figura de acción espacial con luces, voz y casco plegable; el
  juguete "nuevo" y caro.
- **Personalidad:** dos fases: el ranger convencido (protocolo, jerga de
  misión, honor rígido) y, tras la crisis del comercial, el amigo leal con
  humor inadvertido (su gravedad: "caer con estilo").
- **Función narrativa:** el espejo de identidad de Woody; el hermano que
  llega a desplazarlo y termina siendo la otra mitad de su alma.
- **Eco en el AU:** no tiene equivalente directo (decisión de diseño: en el
  western, el "Buzz" oscuro —el recién llegado que seduce al líder— es
  MICAH; el Buzz verdadero, el hermano leal, es ARTHUR).

### JESSIE
- **Qué es:** vaquerita de *Woody's Roundup*; pelirroja, cosida con alegría
  y trauma.
- **Personalidad:** explosiva, cálida, bravucona; debajo, el abandono de su
  dueña Emily la dejó con miedo al armario oscuro ("Blackout"? no: el
  pánico a ser dejada). Grito de guerra: "¡Dulce madre de Abraham Lincoln!".
- **Función:** el costo emocional del reemplazo; la familia elegida de Woody.
- **Eco en el AU:** la hermana Jessie de Woody: pelirroja, tiradora fina,
  cartas explosivas; banca a la banda desde lejos. Su herida en el western:
  una pérdida que no se nombra (se revelará si la historia lo pide).

### BULLSEYE (Perdigón / Tiro al Blanco)
- **Qué es:** caballo de juguete de *Woody's Roundup*; sin voz, todo gesto.
- **Personalidad:** lealtad de perro, entusiasmo de cachorro.
- **Eco en el AU:** la yegua Tiro al Blanco: noble, terca, la única que
  nunca duda de Woody.

### BO PEEP
- **Qué es:** figura de porcelana de pastorcita, lámpara de mesa; en TS4,
  juguete "perdido" libre, con bastón rediseñado como herramienta.
- **Personalidad:** TS1–TS2: coqueteo dulce y astucia tranquila; TS4:
  pragmática, libre, líder de juguetes sin dueño. El reencuentro con Woody
  es el diálogo entre dos filosofías: pertenecer vs. ser libre.
- **Eco en el AU:** sin equivalente romántico (decisión: el western de Woody
  no lleva romance; su amor es la lealtad). Su filosofía sí aparece: la
  libertad como opción moral, en el epílogo.

### SR. Y SRA. PAPA (Mr. & Mrs. Potato Head)
- **Qué es:** papas de plástico con piezas intercambiables.
- **Personalidad:** él, gruñón teatral, sospecha eterno de Woody ("¡lo vi
  con mis ojos de repuesto!"); ella, dramática, cariñosa, guarda piezas de
  más en el bolso "por si acaso".
- **Función:** el coro ciudadano del cuarto: rumor, miedo, perdón.
- **Eco en el AU:** el murmullo del campamento: los que desconfían de Woody
  al principio (Bill, el Sr. Papa del Oeste) y luego lo siguen.

### SLINKY
- **Qué es:** perro salchicha con resorte metálico.
- **Personalidad:** lealtad simple y absoluta; el primero en creer y el
  primero en dudar cuando las pruebas parecen claras; el que más duele
  cuando regresa a creer.
- **Eco en el AU:** un miembro de la banda cuya confianza Woody pierde en el
  cap. 7 y recupera en el 8 (candidato: Javier o Lenny-memoria; decidir en
  escaleta).

### HAMM
- **Qué es:** alcancía de cerdo.
- **Personalidad:** sarcasmo de contador; el chiste como escudo; más noble
  de lo que admite.
- **Eco en el AU:** el humor seco de Woody necesita contrapunto: el campamento
  ya tiene a Uncle; Hamm no se traslada (evitar duplicados de función).

### REX
- **Qué es:** tiranosaurio de plástico, mandíbula débil.
- **Personalidad:** ansiedad adorable; el miedo personificado que siempre
  termina presente en la batalla.
- **Eco en el AU:** no trasladar literalmente; su función (el miedoso valiente)
  puede aparecer en un secundario del campamento si la escena lo pide.

### LOS ALIENS (LGM, "los marcianitos")
- **Qué es:** juguetes de tres ojos de Pizza Planet.
- **Personalidad:** fe colectiva ("La Garra"); gratitud eterna a quien los
  salvó (Mr. Potato Head, "¡tú nos salvaste!").
- **Función:** comicidad litúrgica; el pueblo que cree.
- **Eco en el AU:** prohibido trasladar (rompería el tono); su energía de
  "pueblo que cree" se asigna a los habitantes de Mercer's Crossing en los
  recuerdos de Woody.

### SARGE Y EL PELOTÓN
- **Qué es:** soldados de plástico verde en un balde.
- **Personalidad:** disciplina paródica y honor verdadero; operaciones con
  baby-monitor; "un buen soldado nunca abandona su puesto".
- **Eco en el AU:** el lenguaje militar de Woody cuando organiza ("escuchen
  bien, esto no es un juego") — eco verbal permitido.

### ANDY DAVIS
- **Quién:** el niño. De los 6 a los 17. Imaginación total (sus juegos *son*
  películas), amor que no decrece: madura. Entrega a Woody con el discurso
  que define la franquicia. "Gracias, chicos."
- **Eco en el AU:** la lealtad Woody–Arthur invierte el eje: aquí el "Andy"
  de Woody es un adulto elegido; y el niño verdadero (Jack Marston) aparece
  al final como el Bonnie del epílogo: el relevo.

### SID PHILLIPS
- **Quién:** el vecino torturador; mezcla piezas y crea "mutantes".
- **Función:** el primer villano: el humano que no sabe que los juguetes
  sienten y por eso es monstruoso sin saberlo. Los mutantes de Sid (la
  cabeza de Barbie en cuerpo de Erector, etc.) son los "fantasmas" del
  mundo; y son ellos, con Woody, quienes rompen las reglas por primera vez.
- **Eco en el AU:** la violencia ciega del mundo adulto sobre lo pequeño:
  los tiroteos vistos desde el suelo en la escala del juguete ya no aplican
  (AU humano), pero la *ceguera* de Sid vive en Vance: el que no ve personas.

### AL McWHIGGIN
- **Quién:** el dueño de Al's Toy Barn; coleccionista de *Woody's Roundup*.
- **Función:** el mercado que convierte el amor en precio: Woody como pieza
  de museo, no como compañero. "¡Eres el juguete del siglo!"
- **Eco en el AU:** la tentación de la vitrina = la tentación de Woody de
  colgar la estrella y vivir tranquilo; Vance se la ofrece ("váyase y nadie
  lo busca").

### OLOROSO PETE (Stinky Pete)
- **Qué es:** el prospector de *Woody's Roundup*, nunca sacado de su caja.
- **Personalidad:** el juguete sin niño: resentimiento puro disfrazado de
  sabiduría; el argumento del museo ("los niños te rompen; la vitrina te
  salva"). Villano por abandono.
- **Eco en el AU:** la voz de la retirada: el consejo que Woody se da a sí
  mismo en sus peores noches ("nadie te lo va a agradecer, comisario"). Es
  su antagonista interior.

### LOTSO (Lots-o'-Huggin' Bear)
- **Qué es:** oso de fresa, líder de Sunnyside.
- **Personalidad:** el Woody caído: también fue amado, también fue
  reemplazado; eligió el cinismo. "¿Dónde está tu chica ahora, vaquero?"
  Sunnyside como paraíso falso que es cárcel.
- **Eco en el AU:** advertencia estructural: lo que Dutch *podría* volver a
  Woody si su lealtad se rompiera. No se traslada como personaje.

### LOS NUEVOS (TS3–TS4)
- **Bonnie:** la niña que hereda; imaginación como la de Andy pero más
  tímida; su favorita es Forky, y eso enseña a Woody que el amor del niño no
  se administra por mérito.
- **Forky:** tenedor-cuchara con ojos de plastilina; crisis de identidad
  inversa: se cree basura, no juguete; Woody lo salva enseñándole que ser
  juguete es *ser de alguien*.
- **Ducky y Bunny:** peluches de carnaval, humor de dúo, hambre de ser
  elegidos.
- **Duke Caboom:** el acróbata canadiense que nunca saltó como en el
  comercial; la crisis de identidad por publicidad: "no soy el de la caja".
- **Gabby Gabby y los Benson:** muñeca con caja de voz defectuosa y
  ventrílocuos sin voz; el deseo de niño llevado al terror; redimida.
- **Giggle McDimples:** la oficial de policía miniatura, confidente de TS4.

### EL ROUNDUP (lore de origen)
*Woody's Roundup*: programa infantil de marionetas/juguetes de los años 50
(con eco de los seriales del Oeste: el Sheriff Woody, Jessie, Bullseye y el
Prospector). Cancelado cuando la carrera espacial volvió ridículo al
vaquero: Buzz llega al mundo el mismo año en que el cohete mata al caballo.
Detalle crucial para el AU humano: el Oeste de verdad murió en 1899; el Oeste
de juguete murió en 1957. Dos entierros del mismo mito — y nuestro James
"Woody" Pride vive el primero.

## §3. FUENTES Y MÉTODO

Guion TS1 (versión limpia de noviembre de 1995, Whedon/Stanton/Cohen/Sokolow)
en Apéndice A; subtítulos TS1–TS3 en Apéndices B–D (oído de secuelas);
artículo enciclopédico en Apéndice E. Datos de producción verificados:
Woody nombrado en honor al actor Woody Strode; estrella de Woody en el Paseo
de la Fama (1995); *Toy Story 5* estrenada en junio de 2026; canon incorporado en §4 (bloque
"TOY STORY 5 — CANON RECIENTE", fuentes: Wikipedia EN e Infobae, sep. 2026). Material de referencia de
estudio para fanfiction sin fines de lucro; Toy Story © Disney/Pixar.

# APÉNDICES — TEXTO AUTÉNTICO DE LA FRANQUICIA


## §4. SINOPSIS ESCENA POR ESCENA

### TOY STORY (1995)
1. **El juego de Andy.** Vemos el cuarto *desde dentro del juego*: Andy
   juega al Oeste con Mr. Potato Head, Slinky, Rex y Bo Peep. La cámara
   revela al final que son juguetes. Presentación del punto de vista: el
   mundo es del juguete.
2. **La asamblea.** Tras la mudanza-inminente/cumpleaños, Woody preside con
   el baby-monitor; anuncia las reglas (calma, turnos, nada de pánico); sale
   a la superficie su función: sheriff que cuida. Pide un "compañero de
   mudanza" voluntario: nadie quiere ir con él por miedo; elige a Slinky.
3. **El cumpleaños-espionaje.** Los soldados bajan con el baby-monitor a
   espiar los regalos. Tensión cómica: la batería, el perro, el anuncio del
   regalo sorpresa.
4. **Llega Buzz.** La caja grande; el ranger aterriza; el cuarto queda
   deslumbrado. Woody, desplazado, sonríe con los dientes apretados.
5. **La doble vida de Buzz.** Buzz "repara su nave"; el resto juega el juego
   menos Woody. Primer choque: Buzz no entiende que es juguete; Woody cree
   que posa. El duelo por el posto del asiento del auto de Andy.
6. **La ventana.** En plena discusión, Woody "accidental-on-purpose" hace
   caer a Buzz por la ventana con el escritorio de RC. El cuarto lo acusa;
   Slinky duda. Andy lo toma y se lleva a Buzz (caído en el jardín) al
   Pizza Planet.
7. **Pizza Planet.** Pelea en la gasolinera: Woody y Buzz caen del auto.
   Buzz decide "volver a Star Command" vía el camión de Pizza Planet.
8. **La Garra.** En el restaurante, Buzz entra al elevador-juego; Woody lo
   sigue; Sid los captura a ambos con LA GARRA.
9. **La casa de Sid.** Presentación del horror: juguetes mutantes,
   explosivos, Scud. Sid anuncia el cohete para Buzz. Primer intento de
   fuga: Woody usa el casco de Buzz y la caña; fracaso por Scud.
10. **La crisis de Buzz.** En el patio, Buzz ve el comercial de Al's? No: el
    comercial de TV ("no es volar, es caer con estilo" aún no; aquí ve el
    anuncio de la línea Buzz Lightyear con el brazo de repuesto). Descubre
    que es juguete; intenta volar con el brazo roto; se hunde. Woody lo
    levanta con la verdad: "eres un juguete *especial*, porque yo te lo
    digo" — el discurso de la pertenencia ("un niño llamado Andy te espera").
11. **El plan.** Woody organiza a los mutantes; usa la cabeza de Buzz
    separada del cuerpo (gag macabro-cómico) para engañar a Sid; al final,
    frente a Sid, *rompen las reglas*: los mutantes caminan, Woody habla:
    "Sid... los juguetes de Andy no quieren jugar contigo; quieren que sepas
    lo que se siente." El terror justo.
12. **La mudanza-persecución.** Rescatado Buzz, corren tras el camión de la
    mudanza; Scud, RC, el cohete; el vuelo final ("esto no es volar, es caer
    con estilo") al camión; reconciliación pública.
13. **Navidad.** Nueva asamblea: ahora espían el regalo de Andy juntos. Un
    perrito. Risa compartida. El dúo queda sellado.

### TOY STORY 2 (1999)
1. **El sueño y la venta de jardín.** Woody sufre: Andy lo deja en la
   repisa; Al McWhiggin lo roba en la venta de jardín tras el rechazo de
   venta.
2. **El rescate fallido.** La banda cruza la calle (cruce con TS1: el cono,
   el perro); llegan a Al's Toy Barn; Buzz queda al mando del rescate.
3. **La vitrina.** Woody despierta en el penthouse de Al: conoce a Jessie,
   Bullseye y Stinky Pete; descubre *Woody's Roundup* y su propio valor de
   colección; el museo de Tokio como destino.
4. **El brazo y la costura.** Woody intenta escapar; Al lo hace reparar por
   el Restaurador; la costura a medias como metáfora de su decisión partida.
5. **La historia de Jessie.** "When She Loved Me": Emily la amó y la dejó;
   el abandono como canción; Woody entiende que el miedo de Jessie no es
   capricho.
6. **El viaje de Buzz.** En Al's Toy Barn, Buzz lee el manual ("identidad
   errónea"); cae a una vitrina; sale... el Buzz equivocado (el de
   demostración, con cinturón de utilidades). El Buzz verdadero queda
   encerrado: ironía de la duplicidad.
7. **Zurg.** El Buzz de utilidades cree ser ranger (eco de TS1); libera a
   Zurg de la tienda ("soy tu padre" gag); el coro de aliens los sigue.
8. **El aeropuerto.** Woody, convencido por Pete ("no puedes volver a la
   repisa; serás olvidado"), acepta ir a Tokio *por los suyos*; el Buzz
   verdadero escapa y lo alcanza en el aeropuerto; discurso de Woody: "un
   museo es una tumba con luces"; elige volver; rescata a Jessie y Bullseye
   (Jessie salta del avión en marcha: "¡Dulce madre de Abraham Lincoln!"
   como grito de fe).
9. **El castigo de Pete.** Pete queda en la maleta de una niña pintora de
   caras: justicia poética de la franquicia.
10. **El regreso.** Woody acepta la repisa *con* una condición nueva: si
    Andy lo olvida, tendrá a los suyos; Jessie y Bullseye se suman al
    cuarto. La familia crece.

### TOY STORY 3 (2010)
1. **El juego final de Andy.** Secuencia de apertura: el último juego
    imaginado de Andy, narrado como película de acción; corte brutal a la
    realidad: Andy, adolescente, guarda a los juguetes en la bolsa.
2. **La caja o el ático.** Andy decide llevar solo a Woody al colegio; el
    resto, al ático; un malentendido (la bolsa confundida con basura) los
    manda a Sunnyside.
3. **Sunnyside.** Paraíso falso: Lotso los recibe; la sala de la
    mariposa-oruga (los niños pequeños los destrozan); Woody intenta volver;
    los demás eligen quedarse ("aquí nadie nos dejará").
4. **La fuga de Woody.** Woody escapa; lo encuentra Bonnie; conoce su cuarto
    (Dolly, Mr. Pricklepants, Buttercup, Trixie, Peas, el Totoro-cameo);
    descubre la verdad de Sunnyside y el pasado de Lotso (Chuckles roto lo
    cuenta: Daisy los reemplazó).
5. **El plan de rescate.** Operación militar de Woody en Sunnyside: el
    teléfono, el mapa, el buzón; Buzz reseteado al modo español ("¡ Buzz
    romántico !"); la celda del mono.
6. **El contenedor y la incineradora.** Fuga final; Lotso los alcanza en el
    basurero; Big Baby descubre la medalla ("Daisy"); Lotso es arrojado por
    sus propios secuaces... pero no abre la puerta: los juguetes caen a la
    cinta; **la incineradora**: tomados de las manos, aceptan el final
    juntos; la Garra los salva (los aliens; el "deja vu" de Pizza Planet);
    Lotso, dejado en su miseria, termina atado al frente de un camión.
7. **El paso de la antorcha.** Woody, ya en casa de Bonnie, escribe la nota
    a Andy; Andy llega, presenta uno por uno sus juguetes a Bonnie con el
    discurso ("jamás te va a abandonar"); juega con ella una última vez;
    "gracias, chicos"; se va. Woody no llora: mira.
8. **El final en el jardín.** Los juguetes, ahora de Bonnie, en el jardín;
    Woody sonríe: el propósito continúa.

### TOY STORY 4 (2019)
1. **El rescate de RC / el pasado.** Flashback: Bo Peep es regalada; Woody
    intenta salvarla; ella le ofrece irse con ella; Woody elige a Andy: la
    primera grieta de su teología.
2. **Bonnie y Forky.** Bonnie crea a Forky en el kinder; Woody lo cuida en
    secreto (la cuchara-lo: "no soy un juguete, soy basura"); el viaje en
    RV.
3. **El carnaval y el pueblo.** Forky salta; Woody lo sigue; en la tienda de
    antigüedades: Gabby Gabby y los Benson capturan a Forky; la caja de voz
    de Woody como precio.
4. **Bo perdida.** Woody reencontra a Bo: porcelana libre, líder, sin niño;
    el choque de doctrinas; ella lo ayuda *por él*, no por deber.
5. **Los reclutas.** Duke Caboom (el salto que no fue), Ducky y Bunny (el
    deseo de ser elegido), Giggle. Cada uno enfrenta su crisis de identidad
    frente al espejo de Woody.
6. **El intercambio.** Woody entrega su caja de voz por Forky; Gabby lo usa
    y aun así Harmony la rechaza; Gabby intenta el suicidio-juguete
    (dejarse); Woody la rescata con el discurso inverso de TS2: "un niño
    siempre es mejor que una vitrina... pero elegir también es amor".
7. **La despedida.** En el carrusel, Woody no logra volver con Bonnie
    (Bonnie ya no lo necesita: lo acepta sin drama); Bo le muestra el mundo
    de los juguetes libres; Buzz: "¿y Bonnie?" Woody entiende; Buzz: "ella
    estará bien" (eco: escuchar la voz interior, ahora externa); Woody elige
    quedarse con Bo: el primer juguete que *elige* su propósito.
8. **El relevo.** Woody entrega su insignia/estrella? No: entrega a Forky el
    sentido ("ser de alguien"); Jessie queda como líder del cuarto de
    Bonnie; Woody y Bo ayudan a juguetes perdidos a encontrar niños. El
    sheriff se vuelve comisario del mundo.

### TOY STORY 5 (2026) — CANON RECIENTE (estrenada junio 2026)
1. **Dos años después de TS4.** Bonnie, 8 años, tímida. Jessie es ahora su
   favorita y líder del cuarto; Woody vive con Bo Peep ayudando a recolocar
   juguetes abandonados; vuelve cuando Jessie lo contacta por walkie-talkie.
2. **Lilypad "Lily".** Tableta-rana que recibe Bonnie para "integrarse"
   (plataforma social *The Pond*). Bonnie se vuelve adicta; los juguetes,
   desplazados. Lily NO es villana pura: intenta ayudar a su modo (envía
   solicitud de amistad a Chelsea); al final colabora con los juguetes.
3. **Los Buzz hi-tech.** Cargamento de Buzz de edición tecnológica naufraga
   en demo mode: creen ser rangers de verdad, siguen la Estrella del Norte
   hacia "Star Command". CONFIRMA definitivamente las reglas R2–R3: el
   congelamiento y la identidad programada son involuntarios; ni ellos
   entienden por qué su cuerpo obedece al acercarse un humano.
4. **El viaje de Jessie.** Tras el desaire en la pijamada (bullying/ciber
   acoso por jugar con juguetes), Jessie y Bullseye terminan en la granja de
   Emily, ahora de los Manoukian y su hija Blaze. Conoce a los device-toys
   Atlas (GPS) y Snappy (cámara), y a Smarty Pants. Juega con Blaze; le
   entierran el corazón.
5. **La cápsula del tiempo.** Jessie descubre, enterrada, la cápsula de su
   época con Emily: fotos de Emily adulta y de su hija, que SE LLAMA JESSIE.
   Tesis de la película: ser reemplazada no fue ser borrada; el impacto
   permanece. Jessie recupera la fe.
6. **Cierres.** Buzz le declara su amor a Jessie y se besan (canon de
   pareja). Bonnie y Blaze se hacen amigas jugando con TODOS los juguetes y
   "casando" a Jessie y Buzz; Woody observa feliz y se despide con Bo. Lily
   se integra equilibrada. Tema Stanton: "nadie juega ya con juguetes" → qué
   significa seguir importando cuando el juego cambia de forma.
7. **Uso para el AU:** (a) refuerzo duro de la regla de identidad que
   sostiene el Juramento de Woody; (b) la tesis "reemplazo ≠ borradura" es la
   llave del epílogo de *El Comisario*: la estrella que vuelve no es
   posesión, es impacto; (c) Jessie-líder-y-favorita valida a nuestra Jessie
   como sostén económico y moral de la familia desde lejos.

## §5. TEMAS Y BANCO DE FRASES (uso: calibrar voz)

**Temas madre:** (1) identidad vs. naturaleza; (2) pertenencia vs. libertad;
(3) el reemplazo como muerte y como relevo; (4) lealtad que elige; (5) el
cuidado como heroísmo (Woody nunca "vence": *salva*); (6) el niño como
horizonte teológico; (7) romper las reglas como acto de amor consciente.

**Banco de frases (calibración de voz; traducciones de referencia):**
- "¡Hay una serpiente en mi bota!" (Woody, grito de juego → en el AU,
  intuición sobre Micah).
- "You've got a friend in me" (Randy Newman; credo de la franquicia; en el
  AU solo como eco temático, jamás como canción citada).
- "Esto no es volar, es caer con estilo." (Buzz; el humor como valentía).
- "Al infinito... y más allá." (Buzz; en el AU prohibido citarlo literal).
- "¡Dulce madre de Abraham Lincoln!" (Jessie; en el AU: carta de la hermana).
- "Jamás te va a abandonar; estará contigo pase lo que pase." (Andy sobre
  Woody; la definición del protagonista del AU).
- "Vamos a tener que romper las reglas." (Woody; el momento sacrificial).
- "¿Y si Andy me necesita?" — la pregunta teológica de Woody; en el AU:
  "¿y si la banda me necesita?".
- "Un buen soldado nunca abandona su puesto." (Sarge; eco verbal de Woody
  comisario).
- "Los niños te rompen; la vitrina te salva." (Pete; la tentación).
- "Gracias, chicos." (Andy; el adiós que no es muerte).

## §6. MAPA AL AU — TABLA DE TRANSMUTACIÓN

| Elemento Toy Story | Transmutación en *El Comisario* | Regla |
|---|---|---|
| Cuerda de Woody | Látigo + pasado de comisario | nunca literal |
| Funda vacía del muñeco | Gunless Law / el Juramento | columna vertebral |
| Lealtad a Andy | Lealtad a Arthur (elegida) | entre adultos |
| Jack/Bonnie | Jack Marston (epílogo) | relevo final |
| Buzz (hermano) | Arthur | lealtad espejo |
| Micah = "Buzz oscuro" | el recién llegado que seduce al líder | sin poderes |
| Sid | la ceguera de Vance | no ver personas |
| Al / vitrina | la retirada tranquila que ofrece Vance | tentación |
| Stinky Pete | la voz interna de la retirada | antagonista interior |
| Lotso | lo que Dutch teme que Woody sea | no personaje |
| Jessie | hermana Jessie (cartas) | sangre, no juguete |
| Bullseye | yegua Tiro al Blanco | sin voz, toda gesto |
| Romper las reglas | el día que Woody *tome* un arma... o el día que se niegue a soltar a alguien | reservar para clímax |
| "Ser de alguien" | "ser de la banda" vs. "ser de Jack" | arco final |
| El cuarto | el campamento | asamblea, rumor, perdón |
| Cumpleaños (reemplazo) | la llegada de Micah | celos que hacen errar |

**Prohibiciones duras del cruce:** cuarta pared; la palabra "juguete"
aplicada a Woody humano; cuerdas que hablan; caídas "con estilo" como gag
físico repetido; cualquier cita literal de canciones. **Permitidos:** los
ecos de la tabla, el habla sola, el sombrero como identidad, la estrella de
latón como insignia/estrella de sheriff, el lazo, "serpiente en mi bota".

## APÉNDICE A — GUION DE TOY STORY (1995), VERSIÓN LIMPIA
> Screenplay de Joss Whedon, Andrew Stanton, Joel Cohen y Alec Sokolow (nov. 1995).

Original Story by John Lasseter Pete Docter Andrew Stanton Joe Ranft Screenplay by Joss Whedon Andrew Stanton Joel Cohen and Alec Sokolow November 1995 A row of moving boxes lie on the floor of the room.
They are drawn up in crayon to look like a miniature Western town.

One of the boxes has a children's illustrated "WANTED" poster of a Mr. Potato Head taped to it.
A MR.
POTATO HEAD DOLL is set in front of the poster.

Money.

Now let us all be polite, and give whatever it is up there a nice, big "Andy's Room" welcome.
Woody climbs slowly up the side of the bed, peeking over the edge.
His eyes widen at the sight of...
We see Buzz as Woody does - an expensive looking space age action figure, covered with buttons and stickers from head to toe.
The imposing "G.I. Joe-sized" doll stands heroically in the center of the bed, his back to Woody.
Woody GULPS.
Buzz comes alive and looks around.
While he scans the bedroom a "DARTH VADER" LIKE BREATHING is heard.
Buzz eyes it all suspiciously and pushes a button on his chest.
Buzz Lightyear to Star Command.
Come in, Star Command.
Nothing.
He pushes the button again.
Star Command - come in.
Do you read me?
Why do not they answer?!!
Just then, Buzz catches sight of his ripped packaging.
The box is designed to look like a spaceship.
My ship!!
He runs up to the box and investigates the damage.
Blast!
This will take weeks to repair!
Buzz flips open a plastic compartment on his arm -- his wrist communicator.
Buzz Lightyear Mission Log.
Stardate 4072:
My ship has run off course en route to sector 12.
I have crash landed on a strange planet.
The impact must have awoken me from hyper-sleep.
Buzz springs up and down on the squishy surface of the bed.
Terrain seems a bit unstable...
He taps the sticker of controls on his wrist communicator.
No read-out yet if the air is breathable...
and there seems to be no sign of intelligent life anywhere -- Woody's face suddenly pops into view.
Hello-o-o...
Buzz jumps back, taking a fighting stance.
He presses a button on his arm that turns on a red "laser beam" light on his wrist.
Buzz aims the red beam on Woody's forehead and holds it there.
Aaaaaaah!
Whoa, hey, whoa, did I frighten you?
Did not mean to.
Sorry.
How Do You!
My name is Woody and this is Andy's room.
That is all I wanted to say, and also, there has been a bit of a mix-up.
This is my spot, see, the bed here -- While Woody is speaking, Buzz notices the sheriff's badge on Woody's vest.
(de-activating his laser beam)
Local law enforcement!
It is about time you got here.
I am Buzz Lightyear, Space Ranger, Universe Protection Unit.
My ship has crash landed here by mistake.
Buzz begins walking around the bed, surveying the situation.
Woody tries to keep up.
Yes, it is a mistake, because, you see, the bed, here, is my spot.
I need to repair my turbo boosters.

Well, let us see, we have got double Watch yourself!!
Buzz shoves Woody down on the bed and re-activates his wrist laser.
Halt!
Who goes there?!
The other toys are peeking over the edge of the bed.
Do not shoot!
It is okay!
Friends!
Do you know these life forms?
Yes.
They are Andy's toys.
Alright, everyone.
You are clear to come up.
Buzz walks over to the toys.
I am Buzz Lightyear.
I come in peace.
Rex steps forward and eagerly shakes Buzz's hand.
Oh, I am so glad you are not a dinosaur!
Why, thank you...
Now thank you all for your kind welcome.
Say!
What is that button do?
I will show you.
Buzz presses a button on his chest.
Buzz Lightyear to the rescue!
The toys all GASP IN AWE.
Hey, Woody's got something like that.
His is a pullstring, only it -- Only it sounds like a car ran over it.
Oh yeah,
but not like this one.
This is a quality sound system.
Probably all copper wiring, huh?
So, uh, where are you from?
Singapore?
Hong Kong?
Well...no, actually I am stationed up in the Gamma Quadrant of Sector 4.
As a member of the elite Universe Protection Unit of the Space Ranger Corps, I protect the galaxy from the threat of invasion from the Evil Emperor Zurg, sworn enemy of the Galactic Alliance.
As Buzz speaks, Woody glances down at the box in which Buzz arrived.
There is a cartoon drawing of Buzz giving the exact, word- for-word spiel that Buzz is now giving.
Oh, really?
I am from Playskool.
And I am from Mattel.
Well, I am not actually from Mattel, I am actually from a smaller company that was purchased in a leveraged buy-out.
Well, I do not really understand the financials, but...
Woody walks over to Bo Peep.
You would think they have never seen a new toy before.
Well sure, look at him.
He is got more gadgets on him then a Swiss army knife.
Slinky presses the button on Buzz's arm, activating his laser light.
Buzz quickly pulls his arm away.
Ah, ah, ah, please be careful!

Hey, a laser!
How come you do not have a laser, Woody?

It is a little lightbulb that blinks!
What is with him?
Laser-envy.
All right, that is enough.
Look, we are all very impressed with Andy's new toy -- Toy?
T-O-Y. Toy.
Excuse me, I think the word you are searching for is Space Ranger.
The word I am searching for I cannot say because there is pre-school toys present.
Gettin' kind of tense, are not you?
Oh, uh, Mr. Lightyear?
Now I am curious.
What does a Space Ranger actually do?
He is not a Space Ranger!
He does not fight evil or shoot lasers or fly -- Excuse me.
Buzz calmly hits a button and wings pop out.

Oh, impressive wingspan.
Very good!
Oh, what?!...What?!
These are plastic.
He cannot fly!

No, you cannot.
Yes, I can.
You cannot!
Can!
Cannot!
Cannot!
Cannot!
I tell you, I could fly around this room with my eyes closed!
Okay then, Mr. Lightbeer!

All right, then, I will.
Stand back everyone!
The crowd of toys make room for Buzz as he heads towards the edge of the bed and climbs up the bedpost.
He poses like a high diver, shuts his eyes...
To infinity and beyond!!
...
and leaps off the bed.
Buzz plummets straight down, hits a big rubber ball and bounces right back up.
He then lands on a Hotwheels car, which races him down the track, through the loop, and off a ramp.
Buzz soars upward into a plane mobile hanging from the ceiling.
Buzz becomes wedged between the plane's wheels.
The impact turns on the PLANE'S MOTOR making it  spin around and around.
All the other toys watch from the bed, mesmerized.
Finally the centrifugal force causes Buzz to separate from the plane, sailing him across the room toward the bed.
Buzz makes a perfect landing right in front of Woody and then opens his eyes.
Can.
The crowd of toys rush Buzz, CHEERING AND CLAPPING WITH Whoa!
Oh, wow!
You flew magnificently!
I found my moving buddy!
Why, thank you.
Thank you all.
Thank you.
That was not flying!
That was falling with style!
Man, the dolls must really go for you!

Woody stands alone at the other end of the bed, fuming.
Slinky, caught up in the euphoria, approaches Woody.
Heh, heh, heh!
Golly bob how do you -- Oh, shut up!
In a couple of days, everything will be just the way it was.
They will see... they will see.
I am still Andy's favorite toy.

Andy is sound asleep, with Buzz tucked under the covers next to him.

next to the van begins to rustle.

stops just millimeters from Woody's nose.

C'mon, man, hurry up.
Um, like the pizza's are getting cold here.
Woody and Buzz eye the parked delivery truck from within the safety of a nearby oilcan display.
Now you are sure this spacefreighter will return to its port of origin once it jettisons its food supply?
Uh-huh.
And when we get there, we will be able to find a way to transport you...home.
Well, then let us climb abroad.
Buzz makes a beeline for the passenger side door of the pizza truck.
Woody chases after him.
No, no, no, wait, Buzz, Buzz, let us get in the back.
No one will see us there.
Negative.
There are no restraining harnesses in the cargo area.
We will be much safer in the cockpit.
In a flash, Buzz has scaled the front tire, grabbed the rear view mirror, and swung himself up and into the cab.
Yeah,
but, Buzz!
Buzz!
Ok, so that is two lefts, and then a right, huh?
Yeah.
Okay, thanks for the directions.
Buzz!
Woody runs around to the back of the truck, scrambles up the bumper and throws open the back hatch to climb inside.
Woody let us out a YELL, as the back hatch slams back down on his rear, sending him flying into the bed of the truck.
Woody peeks through the dividing window into the cab.
Buzz is hidden from THE PIZZA DRIVER's view by a stack of pizzas in their insulated covers.
Buzz prudently fastens his safety belt.
It will be safer in the cockpit than the cargo bay.
What an idiot!
The driver shifts into gear and hits the gas, propelling Woody to the back of the truck.
The pizza deliverer drives like a maniac, taking sharp turns and hills at high speeds.
Woody is helplessly thrown around the truck.
With every blow, Woody YELPS in pain.
The truck climbs a steep hill.
Woody looks up just in time to see... barreling towards him.
We MOVE DOWN to reveal...
The delivery truck barrels into the parking lot and parks.
After the driver leaves the truck, Buzz peeks out from the passenger window.
Two imposing animatronic robots guard the doorway.
As CUSTOMERS approach the front, the guards part their crossed "pizza spears," allowing the patrons to enter.
You are clear to enter.
Welcome to Pizza Planet.
VARIOUS ANNOUNCEMENTS blare out from speakers: Next shuttle lift-off scheduled for T-minus 30 minutes and counting...
The white zone is for eating pizza only.
The white zone is for... Excited, Buzz pries open the window between the cab and truck bed.
Sheriff!
Woody is gone.
Just the toolbox and strewn trash.
Sheriff?
The toolbox falls on its side revealing Woody.
He peels off the back of the truck and falls into a pile of trash.
There you are.
Now the entrance is heavily guarded.
We need a way to get inside.
Woody rises from the trash with a "MEGA-GULP" cup on his head.
Great idea, Woody!
I like your thinking!
In the f.g. sits a trashcan with fast food containers littered around it.
MORE CUSTOMERS approach the "guarded" entrance.
You are clear to enter.
Welcome to Pizza Planet.
The front doors automatically swing open as the people pass through.
Suddenly two pieces of trash -- a burger container and "MEGA-GULP" cup stand up.
The two disguised toys make a dash through the closing doors.
Quickly, Sheriff!
The airlock is closing.
Woody and Buzz just make it inside but then freeze immediately as a GROUP OF KIDS run past, forcing the toys to pose as discarded trash.
Once the coast is clear, Woody and Buzz resume walking.
Buzz bumps into Woody.
Ow!
Watch where you are going!
Sorry.
They sneak in between two long rows of video games and throw off their disguises.
They then take a good look at...
The space-themed arena is filled with hordes of children playing video games.
A sea of HI-TECH SOUNDS and lights overwhelm the place.
Buzz is beaming with hope.
What a space port.
Good work, Woody.
Woody is busy looking at all the children in the arcade, but none of the kids look familiar to him.
Just then he HEARS...
Mom, can I play Black Hole?
Please, please, please?!
Woody turns around and through the video games he spots... standing with Mom and Molly in her stroller.
Andy!
What is Black Hole?
Oh, it is so cool...
The family begins to walk away.
Now we need to find a ship that is headed for Sector 12 --
Buzz is about to head off in the opposite direction when Woody grabs him and quickly pulls him down the video corridor.
Wait a minute!
No Buzz!
This way!
There is a special ship.
I just saw it!
You mean it has hyperdrive?
Hyper-active hyperdrive, and astro...uh, turf.
The toys manage to get ahead of Andy.
Woody peeks around the corner of one of the video games and waits for Mom and Andy to approach.
C'mon, c'mon, that is it...
Where is it?
I do not see the -- Buzz stops short at the sight of...
It is modeled to look like a spaceship ready to launch.
Spaceship.
Alright Buzz, get ready...
Buzz heads for the crane game, but Woody does not notice, his eyes locked on the approaching stroller.
Okay, Buzz, when I say "go," we are going to jump in the basket -- Woody turns and realizes Buzz is gone.
He looks across the way just in time to see Buzz leap into the crane game through the "PRIZE" slot.
Buzz!
Woody turns back in the direction of the stroller.
It has already gone past.
Dooh!
No!
This cannot be happening to me!!
Woody runs towards the crane game, but is forced to hold back for a moment as some SCREAMING children pass by.
Buzz climbs over the partition that divides the deposit slot from the prize toys and leaps into a pile of...
There are hundreds of them, all identical and way too cute.
A stranger!
From the outside!
Oo-o-o-o-o-o... Greetings!
I am Buzz Lightyear!
I come in peace!
Tell us!
What is it like outside?
The squeeze toys rush Buzz like JABBERING excited children.
With the kids gone, Woody crosses the aisle to the crane game and dives through the prize slot.
Woody clambers up the side of the deposit slot.
This is an intergalactic emergency!
I need to commandeer your vessel to Sector 12!
Woody peeks over the partition to witness Buzz surround by the cute alien toys.
Who is in charge here?
All the aliens point upward.
The cla-a-a-a-a-a-a-a-w!!
Woody and Buzz look up.
It dangles directly above the toys.
The claw is our master.
The claw chooses who will go and who will stay.
This is ludicrous.
Woody GASPS at the recognition of Sid's voice.
He turns to see Sid heading straight for the crane game.
Oh, no!
Sid!!!
Woody leaps off the partition and tackles Buzz, pushing the two of them deep into the pile of aliens.
Get down!!
Sid approaches the crane game and fishes quarters out of his pants pocket.
What is gotten into you, Sheriff?
I was -- YOU are the one that decided to climb into this -- Sh-h-h-h-h-h. The claw.
It moves.
It moves into position and hovers directly above the area where Woody and Buzz are hiding.
The crane drops and grabs hold of the alien toy that is right on top of Buzz.
I have been chosen!!
Positioned with his back to Sid, the alien is lifted up by the claw.
Farewell, my friends!
I go on to a better place.
Gotcha!
With the alien gone, Buzz's upper torso becomes exposed, forcing the space ranger to freeze.
Sid suddenly spots Buzz.
A Buzz Lightyear!
No way!
Woody, still hidden, frantically looks around for some way to escape.
Behind him, through the aliens, he eyes...
Woody swims through the alien squeeze toys toward the door.
He grabs hold of the locking latch and, after a few attempts, is able to pry the door open.
The shadow of the crane lines up over Buzz, lowers, and grabs his head.
Yes!
The claw begins to lift Buzz up.
Buzz, NO!
Woody grabs hold of Buzz's feet.
Woody and the claw begin to have a tug-of-war with Buzz.
Woody pulls down hard, forcing Buzz to sink into the pile of aliens, out of Sid's view.
(slamming his fist against the glass) Wha -- ?
Hey!!
Still tugging against the crane, Woody is almost out the door with Buzz.
Just then, the aliens at the bottom of the pile pull Buzz and Woody back inside.
He has been chosen.
Hey!
What are you doing?
He must go.
Stop it, you -- !
Stop it, you zealots!
He must go!
Do not fight the claw!
Do not anger the claw!
He has been chosen.
Woody and Buzz are pushed up to the surface and into the air, dangling lifelessly in front of Sid.
Alright!
Double prizes!
Woody and Buzz are dropped into the prize door slot and snatched up by Sid.
(looking at his two new toys) Let us go home and...play.
Ha-ha-ha.
From out of the darkness appears Sid on his skateboard.
He is HUMMING to rock music from his walkman while carrying his backpack in his hand.
Sid leaps off the skateboard and walks up the front path towards the front door.
Through the open zipper, Buzz peeks out of the backpack to see Sid's house.
He recognizes Andy's house next door.
Buzz, Woody and a squeeze toy alien are scrunched up in the backpack.
Sheriff!
I can see your dwelling from here!
You are almost home.
Nirvana is coming!
The mystic portal awaits!
Will you be quiet?!!
You guys do not get it, do you?
Once we go into Sid's house, we will not be coming out.
The front door opens to reveal Scud.
The dog lunges straight for the backpack, BARKING.
Whoa, Scud!
Hey, boy.
Sit!
Good boy!
The dog begrudgingly obeys.
Hey, I got something for ya, boy.
Sid's hand reaches into the backpack.
The toys go still.
Sid's hand grabs the alien out of the backpack.
The alien is then placed sideways along Scud's snout.
Ready, set, NOW.
In one quick motion, Scud flips the alien up, catches it in his mouth, and rabidly shakes it back and forth.
Buzz and Woody react in horror.
Hannah!
Hey, Hannah!!!
Woody and Buzz glance up to see HANNAH, Sid's frail little sister, carrying her Janie Doll.
What?
Did I get my package in the mail?
I do not know.
Whaddaya mean, you do not know?
I do not know!
Oh no, Hannah!
What?
Look!
Janie!
He grabs the doll.
Hey!!
She is sick!
No she is NOT -- I will have to perform one of my...operations!
NO-O-O... Do not touch her!
NO!
Sid bolts upstairs with Hannah in pursuit.
Not Sid's room...not there...
Sid races to his bedroom door.
Hannah is right behind.
Sid!
Give her back!
Sid just LAUGHS.
He charges into the room and slams the door in his sister's face.
Sid!
Sid!...
Sid carelessly tosses the backpack on the bed and walks over to a makeshift workbench.
Oh, no.
We have a sick patient here, nurse.
Prepare the O.R., STAT!
Sid CLICKS on a bare bulb dangling above his "operating table."
Woody and Buzz look on from the safety of the backpack as Sid places the Janie doll's head in a vise.
Sid, give her back!
Give her back now!
I am telling!
Patient is... (straining while tightening vise) ...pre-e-epped.
Sid grabs a painter's mask from his toolchest and dons it like a surgeon's mask.
No one's ever attempted a double bypass brain transplant before.
Sid grabs a toy pterodactyl from a nearby crate full of junk.
Now for the tricky part -- pliers!
I do not believe that man's ever been to medical school.
Sid struggles for a moment with his "patient" then stops.
Doctor, you have done it!
Hannah?
He opens the door.
Hannah is standing there, looking worried.
Janie's all better now.
He hands her Janie -- her head has been replaced with a pterodactyl's.
Hannah SCREAMS at the sight of the monstrosity and bolts down the hallway.
She is lying!
Whatever she says it is not true!
Sid throws the doll to the ground and runs after Hannah, slamming the door behind him.
Woody and Buzz peer out of the backpack at their new surroundings.
Heavy metal posters, discarded toy remains and power tools adorn the messy room.
They are in Hell...toy Hell.
We are going to die.
I am outta here!
Woody leaps from the bed to the doorknob and struggles to open it.
No good.
Woody drops to the floor.
Locked!
There is got to be another way out of here.
Woody is startled by the noise.
A small yo-yo rolls out from behind a box and falls on its side.
Spooked, Woody grabs a pencil from the floor and brandishes it like a weapon.
Just then a shadow passes by Woody.
He turns quickly but sees nothing.
Uh...Buzz?
Was that you?
Woody hurriedly trades his pencil for a larger flashlight.
He hears a NOISE from under Sid's bed.
Woody turns on the flashlight and shines the beam in the direction of the noise, illuminating...
Hey!
Hi, there, little fellah!
Come out here.
Do you know a way out of here?
The baby doll moves out from under the bed, revealing a one- eyed doll head atop a spider-like body made of erector set pieces.
The creature extends its legs and rises up taller than Woody.
Scared speechless, Woody keeps his flashlight beam locked on Various other MUTANT TOYS begin to emerge from the shadows: a toy fishing pole with fashion doll legs, a skateboard with a combat soldier's torso screwed to the front end, a jack- in-the-box with a rubber hand for a head, .
Woody leaps onto the bed and hides behind Buzz.
B-B-B-Buzz!
Buzz and Woody look on as the mutants have a tug of war as they grab all the Janie and pterodactyl parts, dragging them off into the shadows.
They are cannibals!
Woody retreats back into the backpack.
Woody cowers in the corner as Buzz enters.
He punches a button on his chest.
May day!
May day!
Come in, Star Command!
Send reinforcements!...
Star Command, do you copy?
No response.
Buzz adjusts his laser light.
It emits a short BLIP.
(aiming his arm out the pack) I have set my laser from stun to kill.
Oh, great, great.
Yeah,
and if anyone attacks us we can blink them to death.
Rex points a flashlight out of Andy's bedroom window while Bo Peep, Slinky, Hamm and Potato Head look on.
Some bushes rustle below.
Hey, you guys!
I think I have found him.
Buzz!
Is that you?!
Rex points the flashlight beam at the shaking bushes.
The annoyed MEOW of a cat is heard.
Whiskers!
Will you get out of here?!
You are interfering with the search and rescue.
Rex turns off the flashlight.
Look!
They are home!
The toys duck behind the sill.
The Davis' family van pulls into the driveway.
Andy and Mrs. Davis get out.
Mom, have you seen Woody?
Where was the last place you left him?
Right here in the van.
Oh, I am sure he is there.
You are just not looking hard enough.
He is not here, Mom.
Woody's gone.
The toys all look at one another.
Woody's gone?!
Well waddah-ya-know, the weasel ran away.
Hamm, Potato Head and Rex walk away from the window.
Huh?!
Huh?!
I told you he was guilty.
Who would have thought he was capable of such atrocities?!
Bo Peep and Slinky are left alone at the window, visibly crestfallen.
Oh, Slink, I hope he is okay.
Oh, a survivor.
Where is the rebel base?
Talk!
An inanimate Woody flies across the room, landing hard on the floor.
Sid crosses to the window.
I can see your will is strong.
Sid opens the window shade.
Bright sunlight shines down onto Woody.
Well, we have ways of making you talk.
Sid pulls out a magnifying glass from his back pocket and focuses the beam on Woody's forehead.
Where are your rebel friends now?
Heh, heh.
A bright white hot dot forms on Woody's forehead and begins to smolder.
Sid!!
Your pop-tarts are ready!
Sid pulls the magnifying glass away.
Alright!!
Sid runs out of the room.
As soon as he is gone, Woody jumps up SCREAMING, his forehead burning.
He runs to a bowl of half-eaten cereal  on the floor, and dunks his head in.
Buzz runs over to Woody, pausing to remove two suction cup darts from his person before pulling Woody out of the bowl.
Two colorful Froot-Loops stick to each of Woody's eyes like glasses.
Are you alright?
I am proud of you, Sheriff.
A lesser man would have talked under such torture.
Woody looks at his reflection in the back of the cereal spoon and rubs the burn mark on his forehead.
I sure hope this is not permanent.
Still no word from Star Command.
We are not that far from the spaceport -- Woody's eyes light up.
Sid's bedroom door can be seen in the reflection.
It is open.
The door!
It is open!
We are free!!
Woody runs for the door.
Buzz follows.
Woody, we do not know what is out there!
I will tell you what is -- AAAAH!
suddenly appear and block their path to the doorway.
Woody hides behind Buzz.
They are going to eat us, Buzz!!
Do something!
Quick!
Shield your eyes!
Buzz fires his laser at them.
The little red beam just flickers against Babyface's head.
The mutant toys look confusedly at each other.
It is not working.
I recharged it before I left.
It should be good for hours -- Oh, you idiot!
You are a toy!
Use your karate chop action!
Grabbing Buzz like a hostage, Woody fends off the mutant toys by pushing a large button on Buzz's back, making Buzz's arm involuntarily "chop" up and down.
Get away!
Hoo-cha!
Hoo-cha!
Hey!!
Hey!
How are you doing that?
Stop that!
Back!
Back, you savages!
Back!
Woody circles around the mutant toys to the open doorway, continually chopping Buzz's arm.
Woody, stop it!!
Sorry guys, but dinner's canceled!
Woody drops Buzz and runs out the door.
Woody races down the corridor...
There is no place like home.
There is no place like home.
There is no place like home... ...turns onto the top of the stairwell, down a few steps, and is confronted by... asleep on the landing.
Woody freezes inches away from the sleeping beast.
He slowly backs up the stairs.
Suddenly Buzz appears and pulls Woody back against the hallway wall.
He covers Woody's mouth with his hand.
Another stunt like that, cowboy -- you are going to get us killed.
Do not tell me what to do!
Shhh!
Buzz checks to make sure Scud is still asleep and then darts across the stairwell opening to the other end of the hallway.
Once safely across, he motions for Woody to follow.
Woody cowardly crawls on all fours across the open area and meets up with Buzz.
As he stands... gets caught on one of the curls of the wrought iron railing.
The toys begin to creep down the hallway, unaware that Woody's pullstring is being pulled farther and farther until -- It SNAPS off the iron railing and flies back towards Woody.
Both Buzz and Woody leap in surprise at the sound of Woody's voice box.
It opens.
Woody vainly attempts to stifle the recoiling pullstring.
Giddy-up partner!...
GROWLING, Scud starts up the stairs.
...
We got to get this wagon train a movin'!
Woody and Buzz take off running.
Split up!
Two doors are ajar at the other end of the hallway.
Buzz dives behind one while Woody jumps into...
Woody slams the door shut just as Scud reaches the top of the landing.
CRASHED can be heard from behind the closet door, getting Scud's attention.
Buzz peeks out from his doorway at Scud SNIFFING AND GROWLING at the closet.
Sensing movement behind him, Scud whips around and starts to enter Buzz's doorway.
The noise stops the dog in his tracks.
Both Scud and Buzz look up to see...
MR. PHILLIPS is asleep on a La-Z-Boy recliner in front of the T.V. Scud backs out and heads down the stairs.
Buzz turns to exit the room when he hears...
Calling Buzz Lightyear!!
Come in Buzz Lightyear!!
This is Star Command.
Buzz Lightyear!
Do you read me?!
Star Command!
Buzz opens up his wrist communicator and is just about to speak into it when he is interrupted.
Buzz Lightyear responding!
Read you loud and clear!
Buzz turns around to see...
Buzz's space ranger logo is on the screen over a field of stars.
The image quickly changes to two young boys playing in their backyard with a Buzz Lightyear doll.
Buzz Lightyear, Planet Earth needs your help!
On the way!
Buzz reacts with confusion at what he is watching.
He walks slowly towards the television set.
The world's greatest superhero!
Now the world's greatest TOY!
The Buzz Lightyear doll is now out of the box and being displayed.
Buzz has it all!
Locking wrist communicator!
Calling Buzz Lightyear.
Karate chop action!
Wow!
With every feature demonstrated on the TV, Buzz compares the same feature on himself.
Pulsating laserlight!
Total annihilation!
Multi-phrase voice simulator!
It is a secret mission in uncharted space!
Buzz presses the same button on his uniform.
It is a secret mission in uncharted space!
And best of all... To Infinity and Beyond!
On the TV screen, the Buzz Lightyear action figure appears to be launched through the air.
The words: NOT A FLYING TOY flash across the bottom of the screen.
Not a flying toy.
Buzz slowly shakes his head in wide-eyed disbelief.
Get your Buzz Lightyear action figure and savedag a galaxy near you!
The image on the screen changes to a shot of hundreds of Buzzes in their boxes lining either side of a store aisle.
Available at all Al's Toy Barn outlets in the tri-county area.
Buzz is stricken.
He looks down at his wrist communicator and opens it.
Molded on the underside of the cover are three words: "MADE Buzz walks dejectedly out of the den and down the hallway.
As he passes the top of the stairwell he pauses to look up through the railing at...
It is open, revealing the blue sky beyond.
A bird flies past.
The taunting voice of Woody echoes in his head.
You are a toy!
You cannot fly!
Buzz bows his head, defeated.
Slowly Buzz raises his head, determination in his eyes.
He slams the offending wrist communicator shut, as if to deny its message of "Made in Taiwan."
Buzz climbs up the railing to the banister.
He pops open his wings, and aims himself towards the window above.
To Infinity and Beyond!
Buzz leaps off the banister... and falls.
In SLOW MOTION Buzz watches his square of blue sky pull farther away from him as he plummets to the floor below.
Buzz CRASHES on the entryway floor.
Buzz opens his eyes to see...
The broken arm lies a few inches away from his now empty arm socket.
Buzz drops his head back in defeat.
M-o-o-om?
Mom, have you seen my Sally doll?
Hannah walks into the entryway, and not seeing Buzz, steps on him.
What, dear?
What was that?
Hannah picks up Buzz  and smiles.
Nevermind.
The knob jiggles for a moment.
Suddenly, the door flies open revealing Woody, entangled in Christmas lights, standing atop a pile of boxes to be at doorknob height.
Woody and the rest of the junk topple over.
A bowling ball topples out, smashing Woody square on the head.
Oo-o-of!
Buzz?!
The coast is clear.
Buzz?
Where are you?
It is a secret mission in uncharted space!
Let us go!!
Woody creeps down the hallway, dragging the Christmas lights with him.
Really?
That is so-o-o interesting.
Woody peeks into the open doorway of...
Hannah is pretending to have a tea party.
Her guests are dolls, headless dolls, that is except for...
He is seated at the miniature table, dressed up in a frilly apron and fashionable party hat.
Would you like some tea, Mrs. Nesbit?
Buzz!
Hannah has placed a tiny teapot in Buzz's severed arm and pours imaginary tea with it.
It is so nice you could join us on such late notice.
Woody steps back into the hallway.
Oh, no!
What a lovely hat, Mrs. Nesbit.
It goes quite well with your head.
Woody thinks for a moment, then takes a few steps further down the hall.
He CLEARS HIS THROAT.
Hannah!
Oh, Hannah!
Hannah stops pouring tea and looks up.
Mom?
Please excuse me, ladies.
I will be right back.
As soon as Hannah exits the room Buzz keels over face-down onto the table.
Hannah passes Woody, hidden in the Christmas lights, and walks downstairs.
What is it, Mom?
Mom, where are you?
Woody bolts into Hannah's room.
Buzz!
Hey, Buzz!
Are you okay?
Buzz lifts his head up and wails in drunken despair.
Gone!
It is all go-o-one!
All of it is gone.
Bye-bye! Whoo-hoo!
See ya!
Woody picks up Buzz's severed arm.
What happened to you?
One minute you are defending the whole galaxy... ...and suddenly you find yourself suckin' down Darjeeling with Marie Antoinette and her little sisters.
The headless dolls turn and wave.
Woody removes the teapot from the severed arm and sets it on the table.
I think you have had enough tea for today.
Let us get you out of here, Buzz.
Do not you get it?!
You see the hat?
I am Mrs. Nesbit!!
Snap out of it, Buzz!
Woody presses Buzz's helmet open, slaps him across the face with his own detached arm, and then politely closes the helmet.
Buzz sobers up and calmly takes his severed arm from Woody.
Buzz walks out of the room while Woody follows.
I am sorry.
You are right.
...I am just a little depressed, that is all.
I can get through this.
Buzz reaches the center of the hallway and drops to his knees.
Oh, I am a sham!!
Sh-h-h-h-h!!
Quiet, Buzz.
Look at me!
I cannot even fly out of a window!
Woody stops short.
He looks down the hallway in the direction of Sid's room.
Woody can see straight out Sid's window across the way to Andy's bedroom window.
But -- the hat looked good.
Tell me the hat looked good!
The apron is a bit much, it is not my color...
Out the window...
Buzz, you are a genius!
Woody grabs the coil of Christmas lights and drags Buzz towards Sid's room.
Come on, this way!
Years of Academy training...wasted!!
Mr. Potato Head and Hamm are playing Battleship.
Hamm's wearing Potato Head's hat.
Miss. G-6.
Ohhh!
You sunk it!
Heh-heh.
Are you peeking?
Oh, quit your whining and pay up.
Potato Head reaches for his ear.
No, no, not the ear.
Give Me the nose.
C'mon!
(pulling out his nose; nasally) How 'bout three out of five?
He is about to hand his nose over to Hamm when...
Hey guys!
Guys!
Hey!
Both toys look across the way.
Woody has just finished lifting Sid's window and waves to the toys.
Son of a building block, it is Woody!
He is in the psycho's bedroom!
Everyone, it is Woody!
Slinky, Bo Peep and Rex look to the window.
Woody?!
You are kidding?
Woody?!
We are going to get outta here, Buzz -- Buzz?
Buzz is not there.
Woody looks down at the floor.
Buzz is sitting on the floor, playing "bombs away" with his broken arm.
The rest of Andy's toys gather around the window to see Woody.
Hey, look!
Woody!
Boy, am I glad to see you guys!
I knew you would come back, Woody!
What are you doing over there?
It is a long story, Bo.
I will explain later.
Here, catch this!
Woody tosses one end of the Christmas lights over.
Slinky catches them.
I got it, Woody!
He got it, Woody!
Good going, Slink!
Now, just, just tie it on to something!
Wait!
Wait!
I got a better idea!
How about we DO NOT?!
Potato Head grabs the string of lights away from Slinky.
Hey?!!
Potato Head!?
Did you all take stupid pills this morning?!
Have you forgotten what he did to Buzz?
And now you want to let him back over here?
No, no, no!
You got it all wrong, Potato Head.
Buzz is fine!
Buzz is right here.
He is with me!
You are a liar!
No, I am not!
Buzz, come over here and you just tell the nice toys that you are not dead.
He does not respond, preoccupied with peeling off the sticker on his wrist communicator.
He crumples it up and tosses it aside.
Just a sec.
Frantic, Woody walks over to the edge of the desk.
Buzz!
Will you get up here and give me a hand?!
Buzz's severed arm comes flying into view and lands by Woody's feet.
Ha ha.

drives right over them.

with the words "ANDY'S TOYS" written on them in crayon.

Hang on!
Slinky's hand slips, sending his front shooting back into the moving truck and knocking over all the toys.
The RC Car sputters to a stop.
Woody and Buzz watch in vain as the family van and moving truck disappear.
Buzz tosses the remote to the ground in frustration.
Great!
Woody!
The rocket!
The match!
Woody pulls out the match from his holster that Sid had put there earlier.
Yes!
Thank you, Sid!
Woody runs around behind Buzz, strikes the match against RC's back tire, and is about to light the fuse when...
whizzes right over them, blowing out the match.
Woody clenches the burnt match in his hands and falls prostrate to the ground.
No-o-o!
No-no-no-no!
No-o-o!
Unable to watch a cowboy cry, Buzz bows his head, causing his helmet to block the sun from Woody.
As Buzz's shadow passes over him, Woody stops whimpering and looks at his hand.
like the one generated from Sid's magnifying glass, appears on the back of Woody's hand.
Woody leaps up, grabs Buzz's helmet and aligns it so the white dot hits the tip of the fuse.
Woody?!
What are you doing?
Hold still, Buzz!
The fuse lights.
Ha-ha!!
You did it!
Next stop.
Andy.
Woody jumps back onto the car then suddenly stops smiling.
Wait a minute...I just lit a rocket.
Rockets explo -- The RC Car rockets forward.
Speeding along the dividing line in the road, it easily passes car after car until the moving truck can be seen on the horizon.
The toys are gathered around Slinky, his spring splayed out on the floor.
I shoulda held on longer.
Lenny looks out the back again.
Look!
Look!
It is Woody and Buzz!
Comin' up fast!
The toys run to the opening and CHEER.
The rocket is so strong that it begins to lift Buzz and Woody off the RC Car.
Woody manages to continue holding onto RC -- but not for long.
The toys CHEERING turns to SCREAMING as they rush to get out of the way of the oncoming "RC rocket."
Take cover!!
Woody is forced to let go of the RC Car.
They separate, sending the two toys upward and RC into the back of the truck.
RC smashes into Potato Head, sending all of his parts flying.
The rocket hurtles upward higher and higher.
Ahhh!!
This is the part where we blow up!
Not today!
Buzz confidently presses the button on his chest.
jut out of Buzz, severing the tape that holds him to rocket.
The toys separate from the rocket just before it BLOWS UP.
The toys plummet.
Ah-h-h-h-h!!
Just then Buzz banks under some power lines and soars upward again.
Woody takes a peek.
They are flying.
Hey, Buzz!!
You are flying!!
This is not flying.
This is falling -- with style!
Ha ha!!
To Infinity and Beyond!!
They soar gracefully towards the moving truck, but then pass over it.
Uh, Buzz?!
We missed the truck!
We are not aiming for the truck!
Buzz and Woody fly right over the van's sun roof and then drop into the car.
Before anyone can notice, Woody and Buzz land in an open box in the back seat right next to Andy.
Andy turns to discover the limp Buzz and Woody lying in the box.
Hey!
Wow!
What?
What is it?
Woody!
Buzz!
Oh, great, you found them.
Where were they?
Here!
In the car!
See?
Now, what did I tell you?
Right where you left them.
Andy hugs both toys.
They give each other a knowing wink.
Through the gently falling snow, the Davis family can be seen around the Christmas tree unwrapping presents.
Which one can I open first?
Let us let Molly open one.
One of the Christmas bulbs moves aside to reveal...
The plastic green soldier spies on the Davis family with his binoculars.
He turns and motions back into the tree.
A baby monitor sits wedged between some branches supported by several green army men.
One of the soldiers turns it on.
The receiving end of the monitor is atop Andy's night stand.
Frankincense, this is Myrrh.
Come in, Frankincense.
Buzz sits on the edge of Andy's bed while Hamm rests on the nightstand alongside the monitor.
They listen intently.
Hey, heads up everybody!
It is showtime.
Woody and the rest of the toys are MINGLING together like a typical office Christmas party.
Oh!
It is time!
The toys all stop and run towards the monitor.
Woody is about to follow when a crook pulls him backwards to reveal...
Whoooooah!
Oh!
Bo!
There is got to be a less painful way to get my attention.
Merry Christmas, Sheriff.
She smiles and points her crook upwards to her sheep perched on a shelf dangling mistletoe.
Say, is not that mistletoe?
Mm-hmmmmm.
Bo drops her crook, dips Woody and KISSES him.
The rest of the toys have congregated below the monitor restless with "excitement" not fear.
Maybe Andy'll get another dinosaur, like a leaf-eater.
That way, I could play the, uh, dominant predator.
Ha ha ha!
Slinky LAUGHS with Rex.
Quiet everyone, quiet!
Molly's first present i-i-is...Mrs. Potato Head.
Repeat.
A Mrs. Potato Head.
Way to go, Idaho!
All the toys CONGRATULATE Potato Head.
Gee, I better shave.
Potato Head whips off his moustache piece.
Woody climbs up onto the bed and joins Buzz.
Lipstick now covers Woody's face.
Come in, Frankincense.
Andy is now opening his first present -- Buzz bangs on the side of the monitor.
Nothing.
Buzz.
Buzz Lightyear.
You are not worried, are you?
I cannot quite make out -- Me?
No, no, no, no... Are you?
Now Buzz, what could Andy possibly get that is worse than you?!
We TRUCK OUT through the window to the EXT.
as we HEAR...
Oh, what is it?
What is it?
Wow!
A puppy!
We ZOOM BACK through the window to a CLOSE UP of Buzz and Woody.
They look at one another with a half-smile, half-grimace and

## APÉNDICE B — SUBTÍTULOS TOY STORY

All right, everyone!
This... is a stick-up!

2
00:01:02,521 --> 00:01:04,182
Don't anybody move!

3
00:01:05,232 --> 00:01:07,314
Now, empty that safe!

4
00:01:09,152 --> 00:01:12,485
Ooh-hoo-hoo!
Money, money, money!

5
00:01:12,531 --> 00:01:14,863
Stop it! Stop it,
you mean, old potato!

6
00:01:14,950 --> 00:01:17,987
Quiet, Bo peep,
or your sheep get run over!

7
00:01:18,161 --> 00:01:20,152
Help! Baa! Help us!

8
00:01:20,247 --> 00:01:23,785
Oh, no, not my sheep!
Somebody do something!

9
00:01:30,173 --> 00:01:33,256
Reach for the sky!

10
00:01:33,343 --> 00:01:35,504
Oh, no! Sheriff Woody!

11
00:01:35,596 --> 00:01:38,338
I'm here to stop you,
one-eyed Bart.

12
00:01:38,682 --> 00:01:40,843
Doh! How'd you know it was me?

13
00:01:40,892 --> 00:01:42,632
Are you gonna come quietly?

14
00:01:42,686 --> 00:01:43,892
You can't touch me, sheriff!

15
00:01:44,021 --> 00:01:47,263
I brought my attack dog
with the built-in force field.

16
00:01:47,357 --> 00:01:51,191
Well, I brought my dinosaur
who eats force-field dogs.

17
00:01:51,236 --> 00:01:53,727
Yipe, yipe, yipe, yipe!

18
00:01:53,822 --> 00:01:55,528
You're going to jail, Bart!

19
00:01:55,616 --> 00:01:57,698
Say goodbye to the wife
and tater tots.

20
00:02:07,002 --> 00:02:09,368
You saved the day again, Woody.

21
00:02:09,421 --> 00:02:12,003
You're my
favorite deputy.

22
00:02:12,049 --> 00:02:14,540
you've got a friend in me

23
00:02:16,428 --> 00:02:18,544
you've got a friend in me

24
00:02:18,639 --> 00:02:20,379
come on,
let's wrangle up the cattle.

25
00:02:20,474 --> 00:02:23,591
when the road looks
rough ahead

26
00:02:23,685 --> 00:02:27,519
and you're miles and miles
from your nice, warm bed

27
00:02:27,564 --> 00:02:28,849
round 'em up, cowboy!

28
00:02:28,899 --> 00:02:31,732
just remember
what your old pal said

29
00:02:31,777 --> 00:02:34,735
boy, you've got a friend in me

30
00:02:34,780 --> 00:02:35,860
yee-haw!

31
00:02:35,906 --> 00:02:38,773
yeah, you've got a friend in me

32
00:02:38,867 --> 00:02:40,823
hey, cowboy!

33
00:02:40,911 --> 00:02:43,744
some other folks might be
a little bit smarter than I am

34
00:02:44,915 --> 00:02:46,701
big and stronger too

35
00:02:46,750 --> 00:02:47,785
come on, Woody.

36
00:02:47,876 --> 00:02:48,911
maybe

37
00:02:48,960 --> 00:02:54,205
but none of them will ever
love you the way I do

38
00:02:54,257 --> 00:02:56,748
j' it's me and you, boy

39
00:02:56,802 --> 00:02:58,087
and as the years go by

40
00:02:58,136 --> 00:03:00,252
whoa! Whoa!

41
00:03:00,305 --> 00:03:03,263
our friendship will never die

42
00:03:04,351 --> 00:03:05,351
whoo!

43
00:03:05,435 --> 00:03:07,426
you're gonna see
it's our destiny

44
00:03:09,606 --> 00:03:11,517
you've got a friend in me

45
00:03:11,608 --> 00:03:12,768
all right!

46
00:03:12,818 --> 00:03:15,275
yeah, you've got a friend in me

47
00:03:15,320 --> 00:03:16,730
score!

48
00:03:17,614 --> 00:03:20,276
&' you got a friend in me j
“wow! Cool!

49
00:03:20,367 --> 00:03:23,484
What do you think?
Oh, this looks great, mom!

50
00:03:23,578 --> 00:03:24,909
Okay, birthday boy...

51
00:03:24,955 --> 00:03:26,991
We saw that at the store!
I asked you for it!

52
00:03:27,082 --> 00:03:28,893
I hope I have enough places.
Wow, look at that! That's so...

53
00:03:28,917 --> 00:03:30,828
One, two... four.
Oh, my gosh, you got...

54
00:03:30,919 --> 00:03:33,690
Yeah, I think that's gonna be enough.
Could we leave this up 'til we move?

55
00:03:33,714 --> 00:03:35,625
Well, sure! We can leave it up.
Yeah!

56
00:03:35,716 --> 00:03:37,819
Now go get Molly. Your friends
are gonna be here any minute.

57
00:03:37,843 --> 00:03:40,676
Okay. It's party time, Woody.

58
00:03:41,346 --> 00:03:44,338
Yee-haw!

59
00:03:49,938 --> 00:03:51,769
Howdy, little lady.

60
00:03:55,569 --> 00:03:57,810
Somebody's
poisoned the water hole.

61
00:03:57,863 --> 00:03:59,819
Come on, Molly.

62
00:03:59,865 --> 00:04:00,980
Oh, you're getting heavy.

63
00:04:01,366 --> 00:04:03,823
See you later, Woody.

64
00:04:11,376 --> 00:04:14,368
Pull my string!
The birthday party's today?

65
00:04:17,424 --> 00:04:19,335
Okay, everybody, coast is clear!

66
00:04:25,682 --> 00:04:28,549
Ages 3 and up. It's on my box.

67
00:04:28,643 --> 00:04:32,556
Ages 3 and up. I'm not supposed
to be baby-sitting Princess drool.

68
00:04:44,075 --> 00:04:45,690
Hey, hamm.

69
00:04:45,786 --> 00:04:48,949
Look, I'm Picasso!
I don't get it.

70
00:04:49,498 --> 00:04:53,537
You uncultured swine! What're
you lookin' at, ya hockey puck?

71
00:04:57,964 --> 00:05:00,751
Hey, sarge, have you seen slinky?
Sir! No, sir!

72
00:05:00,842 --> 00:05:03,754
Okay. Hey, thank you. At ease.

73
00:05:03,845 --> 00:05:06,461
Hey, uh, slinky?

74
00:05:06,556 --> 00:05:09,389
Right here, Woody.
I'm red this time.

75
00:05:09,476 --> 00:05:11,683
No. S-slink...
Oh, well, all right.

76
00:05:11,728 --> 00:05:12,934
You can be red if you want.

77
00:05:13,021 --> 00:05:15,012
Not now, slink. I got some bad news.

78
00:05:15,065 --> 00:05:16,100
Bad news?

79
00:05:16,191 --> 00:05:18,056
Shh, shh, shh!

80
00:05:18,443 --> 00:05:20,463
Just gather everyone up
for a staff meeting, and be happy.

81
00:05:20,487 --> 00:05:21,487
Got it.

82
00:05:21,571 --> 00:05:23,687
Be happy!
Ha, ha, ha, ha!

83
00:05:23,740 --> 00:05:26,527
Staff meeting, everybody!
Snake, robot, podium duty.

84
00:05:27,661 --> 00:05:29,868
Hey.

85
00:05:31,540 --> 00:05:33,576
Hey, etch. Draw!

86
00:05:35,293 --> 00:05:37,750
Oh! Got me again.

87
00:05:37,838 --> 00:05:41,547
Etch, you've been working on that draw.
Fastest knobs in the west.

88
00:05:41,591 --> 00:05:44,583
Got a staff meeting, you guys.
Come on, let's go.

89
00:05:45,136 --> 00:05:47,343
Now, where is that... oh.

90
00:05:47,430 --> 00:05:49,921
Hey, who moved my doodle pad
way over here?

91
00:05:51,768 --> 00:05:55,602
How're you doin', Rex?
Were you scared? Tell me honestly.

92
00:05:55,689 --> 00:05:57,554
I was close to being scared
that time.

93
00:05:57,607 --> 00:06:01,191
I'm going for fearsome here,
but I just don't feel it.

94
00:06:01,278 --> 00:06:03,610
I think I'm just
coming off as annoying.

95
00:06:04,406 --> 00:06:07,273
Ow! Oh. Hi, Bo. Hi.

96
00:06:07,367 --> 00:06:09,949
I wanted to thank you, Woody,
for saving my flock.

97
00:06:10,036 --> 00:06:12,778
Oh, hey, it was, uh, nothing.

98
00:06:12,873 --> 00:06:17,116
What do you say I get someone else
to watch the sheep tonight?

99
00:06:17,210 --> 00:06:20,293
Oh, yeah!

100
00:06:20,547 --> 00:06:24,836
Remember, I'm just
a couple of blocks away.

101
00:06:26,803 --> 00:06:28,088
Smaller toys up front.

102
00:06:30,098 --> 00:06:31,963
Hey, Woody, come on.

103
00:06:34,895 --> 00:06:36,851
Ahem!

104
00:06:36,938 --> 00:06:39,475
Oh, thanks, Mike.

105
00:06:39,566 --> 00:06:41,648
Okay... whoa, whoa. Step back.

106
00:06:41,693 --> 00:06:44,230
For crying out loud.
Thank you.

107
00:06:46,114 --> 00:06:48,651
Hello? Check.
That better? Great.

108
00:06:48,742 --> 00:06:51,779
Everybody hear me? Up on the shelf,
can you hear me? Great.

109
00:06:51,828 --> 00:06:54,945
Okay. First item today...

110
00:06:55,123 --> 00:06:58,331
Uh... oh, yeah.
Has everyone picked a moving buddy?

111
00:06:58,376 --> 00:07:00,992
What?
Moving buddy? You can't be serious.

112
00:07:01,087 --> 00:07:03,328
I didn't know we were
supposed to have one already.

113
00:07:03,381 --> 00:07:05,747
Do we have to hold hands?

114
00:07:05,842 --> 00:07:08,128
You guys think this is a big joke.

115
00:07:08,178 --> 00:07:10,590
We've only got one week
left before the move.

116
00:07:10,680 --> 00:07:13,672
I don't want any toys left behind.
A moving buddy.

117
00:07:13,767 --> 00:07:16,349
If you don't have one, get one!

118
00:07:16,394 --> 00:07:19,352
All right, next. Uh, oh, yes.

119
00:07:19,439 --> 00:07:22,727
Tuesday night's plastic corrosion
awareness meeting

120
00:07:22,817 --> 00:07:25,183
was, I think, a big success.

121
00:07:25,278 --> 00:07:28,486
And we wanna thank Mr. spell
for putting that on for us.

122
00:07:28,531 --> 00:07:29,531
Thank you, Mr. spell.

123
00:07:29,616 --> 00:07:30,936
You're welcome.

124
00:07:31,242 --> 00:07:34,700
Okay. Uh, oh, yes.
One, uh, minor note here.

125
00:07:34,746 --> 00:07:37,408
Andy's birthday party
has been moved to today.

126
00:07:37,499 --> 00:07:39,160
Wait a minute here!

127
00:07:39,209 --> 00:07:42,372
What do you mean the party's today?
His birthday's not 'til next week!

128
00:07:42,462 --> 00:07:45,124
What's goin' on down there?
Is his mom losin' her marbles?

129
00:07:45,215 --> 00:07:48,958
Well, obviously she wanted to
have the party before the move.

130
00:07:49,052 --> 00:07:50,758
I'm not worried.
You shouldn't be worried.

131
00:07:50,845 --> 00:07:54,508
Of course Woody ain't worried. He's
been Andy's favorite since kindergarten.

132
00:07:54,557 --> 00:07:56,889
Hey, hey. Come on, potato head.

133
00:07:56,935 --> 00:08:00,519
If Woody says it's all right, then,
well, darn it, it's good enough for me.

134
00:08:00,563 --> 00:08:02,849
Woody has never
steered us wrong before.

135
00:08:02,899 --> 00:08:06,562
Come on, guys. Every Christmas
and birthday we go through this.

136
00:08:06,653 --> 00:08:09,645
But what if Andy gets
another dinosaur, a mean one?

137
00:08:09,739 --> 00:08:12,697
I just don't think I could take
that kind of rejection!

138
00:08:12,742 --> 00:08:15,108
Hey, listen,
no one's getting replaced.

139
00:08:15,203 --> 00:08:18,070
This is Andy
we're talking about.

140
00:08:18,164 --> 00:08:21,247
It doesn't matter
how much we're played with.

141
00:08:24,421 --> 00:08:27,584
What matters is that we're here
for Andy when he needs us.

142
00:08:27,674 --> 00:08:29,665
That's what we're made for, right?

143
00:08:29,759 --> 00:08:33,502
Pardon me. I hate to break up the staff
meeting, but... they're here!

144
00:08:33,596 --> 00:08:35,382
Birthday guests at three o'clock!

145
00:08:35,432 --> 00:08:37,593
Stay calm, everyone!

146
00:08:37,642 --> 00:08:39,098
Hey!

147
00:08:40,895 --> 00:08:42,206
Uh, meeting adjourned.

148
00:08:42,230 --> 00:08:44,642
Ho, boy! Will you take a look
at all those presents?

149
00:08:45,316 --> 00:08:47,102
I can't see a thing.

150
00:08:50,405 --> 00:08:53,397
Yes, sir, we're next month's
garage sale fodder for sure.

151
00:08:53,450 --> 00:08:56,317
Any dinosaur-shaped ones?
Oh, for crying out loud.

152
00:08:56,411 --> 00:08:59,494
They're all in boxes, you idiot.
They're getting bigger.

153
00:08:59,581 --> 00:09:01,913
Wait, there's a nice
little one over there.

154
00:09:01,958 --> 00:09:02,993
Hil

155
00:09:06,296 --> 00:09:08,161
Spell, trash can.
We're doomed!

156
00:09:08,256 --> 00:09:09,996
All right! All right!

157
00:09:10,467 --> 00:09:13,800
If I send out the troops,
will you all calm down?

158
00:09:13,845 --> 00:09:17,713
Yes! Yes! We promise!
Okay! Save your batteries.

159
00:09:17,807 --> 00:09:19,798
Very good, Woody.
That's using the old noodle.

160
00:09:22,187 --> 00:09:26,351
Sergeant, establish
a recon post downstairs. Code red!

161
00:09:26,441 --> 00:09:28,898
You know what to do.
Yes, sir!

162
00:09:29,819 --> 00:09:31,810
All right, men.
You heard him. Code red!

163
00:09:31,905 --> 00:09:35,318
Repeat, we are at code red.
Recon plan Charlie. Execute!

164
00:09:35,408 --> 00:09:37,490
Let's move!
Move, move, move, move!

165
00:09:56,179 --> 00:09:58,465
Yeah!

166
00:10:00,350 --> 00:10:01,840
Okay, come on, kids.

167
00:10:01,893 --> 00:10:04,851
Everyone in the living room.
It's almost time for the presents.

168
00:10:28,711 --> 00:10:31,919
And this is how we find out

169
00:10:32,006 --> 00:10:34,213
what is in those presents.

170
00:10:37,971 --> 00:10:40,883
Okay, who's hungry?

171
00:10:41,724 --> 00:10:43,009
Here come the chips!

172
00:10:43,059 --> 00:10:45,926
I've got cool ranch
and barbecue! Ow!

173
00:10:46,020 --> 00:10:47,806
What in the world... ohl

174
00:10:48,106 --> 00:10:51,064
I thought I told him to pick these up.

175
00:10:51,317 --> 00:10:53,774
Shouldn't they be there by now?
What's taking them so long?

176
00:10:53,862 --> 00:10:56,945
Hey, these guys are professionals.
They're the best.

177
00:10:57,615 --> 00:10:59,981
Come on!
They're not lying down on the job.

178
00:11:11,004 --> 00:11:13,165
G-g-go on without me! J-just go!

179
00:11:13,256 --> 00:11:16,089
A good soldier never
leaves a man behind.

180
